set_variable = { sof_sc_total_politics = sof_sc_base_politics }

else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = sof_sc_count value = 0 compare = greater_than } NOT = { OR = { has_idea = sof_sc_bes_politics_2 has_idea = sof_sc_bes_politics_3 } } } sof_sc_bes_politics_apply_1 = yes }
set_variable = { sof_sc_completed_politics = sof_sc_count }
set_variable = { sof_sc_growth_politics = sof_sc_count }
multiply_variable = { sof_sc_growth_politics = 0.02 }
set_variable = { sof_sc_base_politics = 0 }
set_variable = { sof_sc_secondary_politics = 0 }
if = { limit = { has_idea = sof_sc_bes_politics_1 } set_variable = { sof_sc_base_politics = 0.10 } set_variable = { sof_sc_secondary_politics = 0.05 } }
if = { limit = { has_idea = sof_sc_bes_politics_2 } set_variable = { sof_sc_base_politics = 0.20 } set_variable = { sof_sc_secondary_politics = 0.10 } }
if = { limit = { has_idea = sof_sc_bes_politics_3 } set_variable = { sof_sc_base_politics = 0.30 } set_variable = { sof_sc_secondary_politics = 0.15 } }
set_variable = { sof_sc_total_politics = sof_sc_base_politics }

if = { limit = { OR = { has_completed_focus = SOF_GN_TRS_legacy has_country_flag = sof_sc_done_sof_gn_trs_legacy } } add_to_temp_variable = { sof_sc_count = 1 } }
if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = sof_sc_count value = 5 compare = greater_than } has_country_flag = sof_nr_mandate } sof_sc_trs_science_apply_3 = yes }
else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = sof_sc_count value = 2 compare = greater_than } NOT = { has_idea = sof_sc_trs_science_3 } } sof_sc_trs_science_apply_2 = yes }
else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = sof_sc_count value = 0 compare = greater_than } NOT = { OR = { has_idea = sof_sc_trs_science_2 has_idea = sof_sc_trs_science_3 } } } sof_sc_trs_science_apply_1 = yes }
set_variable = { sof_sc_completed_science = sof_sc_count }
set_variable = { sof_sc_growth_science = sof_sc_count }
multiply_variable = { sof_sc_growth_science = 0.02 }
set_variable = { sof_sc_base_science = 0 }
set_variable = { sof_sc_secondary_science = 0 }
if = { limit = { has_idea = sof_sc_trs_science_1 } set_variable = { sof_sc_base_science = 0.10 } set_variable = { sof_sc_secondary_science = 0.05 } }
if = { limit = { has_idea = sof_sc_trs_science_2 } set_variable = { sof_sc_base_science = 0.20 } set_variable = { sof_sc_secondary_science = 0.10 } }
if = { limit = { has_idea = sof_sc_trs_science_3 } set_variable = { sof_sc_base_science = 0.30 } set_variable = { sof_sc_secondary_science = 0.15 } }
set_variable = { sof_sc_total_science = sof_sc_base_science }
add_to_variable = { sof_sc_total_science = sof_sc_growth_science }
add_to_temp_variable = { sof_sc_total = sof_sc_count }
if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = sof_sc_total value = 0 compare = greater_than } NOT = { has_dynamic_modifier = sof_sc_institutional_consolidation } } add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = sof_sc_institutional_consolidation } }
}
